## 9. Milestones and alpha

### 9.1 The server (from the stabilization spine)

- **M-0, Grounding (DONE 2026-10-03):** spikes S-1…S-4 and WU-01 (fixtures).
- **M-1, Foundation:** WU-02…WU-05. Exit means the Jest ACs among AC-F1…AC-F28 are green under `mise run
  test`, and the script-check ACs (AC-F2, F6, F7, F8, F9, F17) are recorded in the work-log with exit
  codes. **Status:** built 2026-10-04: WU-02…WU-05 done, the wave gates passed at `3e03aeb` and `281ce11` (stab worklog:190, :602).
- **M-2, Engine core:** WU-06…WU-08 and WU-22. Exit means AC-E1…AC-E23, AC-E46, AC-E47 and
  AC-E55…AC-E62 are green. **Status:** built 2026-10-04: WU-06…WU-08 at the wave gate `281ce11`, WU-22 at `baa3fc6` (stab worklog:602, :851).
- **M-α, Alpha = the trade vertical.** WU-14…WU-17, WU-23, plus the human smoke WU-20. **Alpha is reached
  when all of these hold:**
  1. `mise run test` and `pnpm build` exit 0;
  2. every AC of M-1 and M-2 is green, and so are AC-T1…AC-T46;
  3. WU-20's checklist is signed off by the user in the work-log. That means a real `find_upgrades` from
     the user's Claude Code, on their build, with their cookie, returns a ranked result or "no upgrade
     within budget", and every listing has a `Buy:` line (a whisper, or a link that opens the listing).

  Alpha does **not** require WU-09…WU-13 or WU-18…WU-19. Tools those units fix may still be on the
  hygiene allowlist (AC-F26).

  **Status:** its units built 2026-10-04 (wave gates `281ce11` and `baa3fc6`). WU-20's coordinator-run part ran 2026-10-05: items 1, 2, 3 (after the user refreshed the cookie), 5 and 8 passed; items 4, 6 and 7 are the user's and aren't recorded, so condition 3, the user's sign-off, isn't recorded (stab worklog:1103–1127, :1149). The post-smoke units WU-24 (D-90) and WU-25 (D-91) were built 2026-10-06 (stab worklog:1129–1149).
- **M-3, Done:** WU-09…WU-13, WU-18, WU-19, WU-21. Exit means every AC in every slice is green, the
  hygiene allowlist **and** the cruft waiver file are both **empty** (AC-G6), and the user signs off WU-21. **Status:** its units built 2026-10-04; the final gate passed at `20e70e6` (89 suites, 1,006 tests; the M-3 checks pass; AC-G4's fresh clone: 76 tools, Life 2,977), stab worklog:1016–1017. WU-21, the user's final acceptance, isn't recorded (still with the user, `docs/build-guide/recon.md`:203).

## 10. Work-unit graph (all slices)

Full blocks (kind, role, produces, consumes, acceptance) are in the slice specs. Edges are functional
dependencies; shared-file contention is handled by the serialized-wiring rule below. "Touches" lists
each unit's main files beyond its implied scope.

The server's units (WU-01…WU-25) name their slice as `stab 01`…`stab 04` (`docs/stabilization/spec/01-foundation.md`
… `04-advice-docs.md`), whose full blocks hold their touches; the build-guide's slices 01, 02 and 03 are
`docs/build-guide/spec/01-server-tools.md`, `02-plan-compiler.md` and `03-pipeline.md`, and slice 04 is
`04-game-knowledge.md` (D-504, WU-149); slices 05, 06 and 07 (`05-compile.md`, `06-pricing.md`, `07-writer.md`;
D-525) are written by WU-167. Each title starts with the unit's status: built (with its merge or lane-head commit),
done (a spike), recorded, human, withdrawn, unused, or approved and not built (amendment 16's M-30).

### 10.1 The server's graph (from the stabilization spine)

Full blocks (kind, depends-on, touches, produces, consumes, acceptance, milestone) are in the slice
specs. The table above is the DAG. Edges are functional dependencies; shared-file contention is handled by
the serialized-wiring rule below.

**Critical path:** WU-02 → WU-03 → WU-04 → WU-05 → WU-06 → WU-07 → WU-08 → WU-17 → WU-23 → WU-20.
WU-22 runs alongside WU-17/WU-23.

**Parallel lanes:**
- WU-14 → WU-15 runs alongside WU-05…WU-08.
- WU-16 runs alongside WU-08.
- After WU-08 and WU-16, the A3/B lane (WU-09 → {WU-10 → WU-13, WU-11, WU-12}) runs alongside WU-17/WU-23.

**Serialized-wiring rule** (vision §6). These files are shared:
- `src/server/toolSchemas.ts`, `src/server/toolRouter.ts`, `src/server/toolMeta.ts`;
- `src/index.ts`, `src/server/createServer.ts`, `src/utils/contextBuilder.ts`;
- `src/handlers/luaHandlers.ts`, `src/handlers/tradeHandlers.ts`, `src/utils/buildCode.ts`;
- `src/pobLuaBridge.ts`, `src/engine/schemas.ts`;
- `pob-api/API/Handlers.lua`, `pob-api/API/BuildOps.lua`;
- `package.json`, `pnpm-lock.yaml`, `jest.config.cjs`, `mise.toml`;
- `tests/integration/contract.test.ts`;
- `tests/fixtures/tool-calls.json`, `tests/fixtures/tool-hygiene-allowlist.json`,
  `tests/fixtures/tool-disposition.json`, `tests/static/poe1-cruft-waivers.json`.

Edits to these files are short wiring steps, merged one unit at a time in DAG order. Concurrent units
rebase before merging.

**Dependency rule.** Only WU-02 changes dependencies (`package.json`/lockfile). The single sanctioned
exception is WU-14 removing `bottleneck`; WU-14 comes after WU-02 in the DAG. Any other dependency need
is HG-3-adjacent: stop and ask the coordinator.

**Implied scope.** A unit's `touches` implicitly include:
- its own new files;
- the test files its ACs name;
- the existing tests of modules it changes or deletes.

## 11. Operational guardrails

### 11.1 The server (from the stabilization spine)

- **Budget and loop guard.**
  - One unit per agent context.
  - If the unit's gate fails with the **same failure signature 3 times in a row**, stop and escalate
    (`blocked:loop`) with the failing output.
  - A unit that needs to touch a file owned by a not-yet-merged unit stops and reports, rather than
    editing it.
- **Identity and secrets.**
  - Agents never read, print, grep or source `.env`, and never set `POESESSID`.
  - Tests run with C-18's setup: credentials scrubbed in `globalSetup`, live `fetch` disabled, temp
    `POB_DIRECTORY` and `XDG_STATE_HOME`, and spawned servers given an explicit safe env.
  - No identity (name, email) in any request, fixture or commit content except git authorship.
  - The User-Agent is D-25's.
- **Network.**
  - Agents may reach GitHub (`mise run pob:fetch`, package registries).
  - They may never reach pathofexile.com or poe.ninja (D-34, HG-1). **amended by D-297 (amendment 3, as D-335 restates it)**: poe.ninja's documented economy endpoints at a polite rate; see §11.2's network rule.
- **Filesystem.**
  - Agents write only inside their worktree and the temp dirs tests create.
  - Probes that start the server or engine run with an explicit temp cwd, never the repo root.
  - The user's builds folder (`/mnt/c/...`) is off-limits (HG-6).
- **Worktree bootstrap** (each new worktree): `mise trust && pnpm install --frozen-lockfile && mise run
  pob:fetch`. A mise command run before `mise trust` fails with rc 1.
- **Commits:** see D-48.

### 11.2 The build-guide (from the build-guide spine)

- **Budget and loop guard.** One unit per agent context. The same gate-failure signature 3 times in a row
  → stop with `blocked:loop` and the failing output. A unit that needs a file owned by an unmerged unit
  stops and reports.
- **Secrets and identity.** Never read, print, grep, cat, source or copy `.env`; never set `POESESSID`;
  worktrees have no `.env`. No name or email in any request, fixture or file content except git
  authorship. The User-Agent is D-25's.
- **Network during the build.** GitHub (`mise run pob:fetch`) and the npm registry, and poe.ninja's
  documented economy endpoints at a polite rate (serial requests, back-off on HTTP 429, nothing fetched
  twice that a cache could hold) (D-297, as D-335 restates it). Never poe.ninja's builds, profiles or
  character API, which its terms close to third parties (D-335); never pathofexile.com, a request carrying
  a cookie or personal data, or any other host (D-34, HG-1 and D-201, as D-297 amends them). Tests reach
  HTTP only through `fixtureFetch` (D-34 unchanged for tests).
- **No headless sessions** for pipeline runs: they are interactive sessions the user starts (D-263).
- **Filesystem.** Write only inside your worktree and temp dirs. Never touch `/mnt/c/...`: the user's
  builds folder and the default guides folder are there (HG-6). Probes that start the server or engine
  run from `mktemp -d`, with `POB_DIRECTORY`, `XDG_STATE_HOME` and `POB_GUIDES_DIRECTORY` at temp dirs.
  Every builds-folder write goes through `buildFileWriter` (C-17).
- **The M-12 run** uses its own server instance: temp `POB_DIRECTORY`, `XDG_STATE_HOME` and
  `POB_GUIDES_DIRECTORY`, `POE_TRADE_DISABLED=true`, `POE_NINJA_DISABLED=true`, web tools denied, and the
  skill in offline mode (D-201) with the coordinator's pre-supplied checkpoint answers (A-28). Never the
  user's registered `pob2` server.
- **Pipeline runtime rules** (M-13 and after): trade and poe.ninja only through the server, from the one
  coordinator (D-112, D-130); research and V4 agents get lookup tools only, never trade or mutating
  tools (D-178, D-231, A-24), and no agent gets a poe.ninja tool beyond the coordinator's
  `get_currency_rates` (D-335); agents
  write only in the current run folder (D-177); the R1b compile and the D1 and D2 gates retry at most
  twice, then the gates escalate, and verification never escalates (D-127, D-268, D-272); fan-out ≤ 3
  (D-161, D-267); fetching patch notes from pathofexile.com needs the user (D-139). **amended by D-444 (amendment 11, as built)**: after a trade-site rate limit or lockout, no step makes a trade-site request until the user approves `retry-pricing` at the next gate; poe.ninja's exchange stays allowed; a resume clears the stop (amendment 11 §12). **amended by D-445 (amendment 11, as built)**: research agents may read published build guides on D-171's hosts, citing them `external | <url>` (amendment 11 §13).
- **Worktrees and commits (D-48).** Bootstrap with `mise trust && pnpm install --frozen-lockfile && mise
  run pob:fetch`, then `git config --worktree commit.gpgsign false`. Commit, rebase, merge, cherry-pick
  and revert with `-c commit.gpgsign=false`; messages `<type>(WU-nn): <what>` with the build handoff's
  `Co-Authored-By` line. Never push; the coordinator sign-rebases with the user (HG-8). Don't use the
  Serena tools (one shared instance points at the wrong checkout).

### 11.3 Since amendment 7 (amendments 8–16)

Guardrails added from amendments 8–16 (16's M-29 built, its M-30 approved, not built), by amendment, each with its source; a change that amends a rule above sits beside that rule as a note instead. "Amendment N §n" is a section of that amendment's file (`docs/build-guide/spec/amendment-N-*.md`); worklog:n and WL:n are lines of `docs/build-guide/worklog.md`; "handoff M-nn" is that milestone's section of `docs/build-guide/build-handoff-amendments.md`; a hash is a signed commit on `main`.

- **Amendment 8, finding the build's engine (M-20).**
  - Running tests (worklog:4590-4597, `132b2fd`): after two session crashes with several lanes' full gates at once, `jest.config.cjs` caps a full run at `maxWorkers: '50%'`; every test call runs at `nice -n 19`; a full gate runs under one shared `flock` lock, one at a time, in the background; targeted suites at `--maxWorkers=4`.
  - No build agent touches the network; the user runs `league-digest.mjs`; the user approved about seven poe2db requests by the coordinator, serial and outside the build lanes (2026-10-18, amendment 8 §18; worklog:4473-4475); all seven were used, three by the monster-modifier probe and four on the ally auras' keyword pages, serial, the server's User-Agent, no cookie (worklog:4481, 4514-4517). **Amended by D-497 (the user, 2026-11-04)**: poe2db requests need no approval, paced so they can't be mistaken for an attack: D-400's pace (serial, at least 3 s apart, the server's User-Agent, no cookie, one retry after 60 s on a 429 or 5xx, nothing fetched twice).
  - The Markdown data entry format (`### <Name>`, `- Id: <id>`) is shared by the digest, the index and the monster table (worklog:4495).

  - Trade: no trade-site call after a pricing stop, within the session (D-422, D-415); the coordinator's league rule makes none. **amended by D-444 (amendment 11, as built)**: the stop lasts until the user approves a retry at the next gate and covers trade-site requests only (the pipeline runtime rules, §11.2).

  - No change to the guardrails.

  - Guardrails, network during the build: WU-112's spike was the one unit allowed network, and only poe.ninja's documented economy endpoints (serial requests, back-off on 429, nothing fetched twice, fixtures built from saved responses; no pathofexile.com request, cookie or `POESESSID`) (amendment 11 §12; handoff M-24); 17 serial requests, all HTTP 200 (worklog:5744)
  - Guardrails: the private guide library the studies came from is not the build's: never read it, copy from it or name its files in the repository (handoff M-24); the reference library is kept outside the repo so third-party text isn't republished (worklog:5641)
  - Guardrails: live tests run in real conditions (amendment 11 §1 quotes the user, 2026-10-24: "The goal is to see whether it can make good builds, not whether it can make good builds in a hermetically sealed box"; the ruling, worklog:5565-5569: live tests run as plain `claude` on the everyday server rebuilt from `main`, with web access and the user's folders, not the sealed launcher)

  - Guardrails (amendment 12 §23): no agent sends a trade request during a build; test servers keep trade off (`spawnServer`) and the pace off; in a live test one trade client at a time, through the session's server, the user told before pricing starts. The 429 log never holds a cookie, `POESESSID` or any request header (AC-K75). The private guide library stays private: a repro reads it locally, nothing of it is committed, no machine path enters the repo (amendment 12 §23; held, worklog:5995). `CLAUDE.md`'s trade paragraph (rewritten by WU-118, worklog:5937–5938): the server enforces the 45 s floor and 7 per 5 minutes, waiting inside the call; background-sleep pacing is gone; the user's approval, one trade client at a time, telling the user before pricing, at most 30 searches without asking and the stop on the first 429, `rate_limited` or `locked_out` stay (amendment 12 §16).
  - Guardrails (pipeline runtime): pricing steps run alone, one trade call at a time (parallel calls queue past the 60 s cap and come back `would_wait`); before a pricing step the runner tells the user that pricing starts, about how many searches, at least 45 s apart, and that their browser shares the limit (amendment 12 §16).

  - Guardrails (amendment 13 §23): no agent sends a trade request during the build; test servers keep trade off; in the live test one trade client at a time through the session's server, the user told first, the pace line checked first. WU-127 reads only the two plans the user granted (HG-6 via HG-27), copied unchanged with sha1s recorded, no machine path. The private library stays private. The guides folder read only for HG-33 (done; nothing else read, WL:6254). The private library stays uncommitted. `vendor/` never modified: the parse fix lives in `pob-api/` (checked at WU-139's gate, WL:6334).

HG-37, the user's install and read check, comes after the merge, from the main checkout. No server tool changes, so no HG-12 grant. Game knowledge's web is poe2db.tw only, at D-497's pace (choice 1). Unchanged: tests never touch the network (D-34); the trade, web, folder and `.env` rules; one full gate at a time, under the lock; builds in worktrees with signing off, finalized with the user (HG-8).

The pin doesn't change (HG-11, HG-2), and the upgrade script runs only on the user's decision. ## 12. Risk register

One table, ordered by ID: the server's R-1…R-14 (stabilization spine §10), the build-guide's R-20…R-47 (build-guide spine §10), amendment 12's R-48…R-60, amendment 13's R-61…R-72 and amendment 14's R-73…R-84, then the unnumbered risks of amendments 8, 9, 10 and 11, numbered R-85…R-112 at this fold in that order, then amendment 15's R-113…R-117 (approved and built 2026-11-06; its §16 rates each with one word, kept in the Likelihood / impact column), then amendment 16's R-118…R-130 (approved 2026-11-08; M-29's R-118…R-122 updated as built at WU-158, M-30's R-123…R-130 not built; its §27 rates each the same way). Each row from an amendment cites its section; a status after the mitigation is the latest the sources record, and "not recorded" means they record none. "Amendment N §n" is a section of that amendment's file (`docs/build-guide/spec/amendment-N-*.md`); worklog:n and WL:n are lines of `docs/build-guide/worklog.md`; "handoff M-nn" is that milestone's section of `docs/build-guide/build-handoff-amendments.md`; a hash is a signed commit on `main`.

| ID | Risk | Likelihood / impact | Mitigation |

The limiter obeys every server rule with margin 1 (C-11). Honest UA (D-25). Minimal requests per call (D-22) |

## 13. Migration and compatibility

### 13.1 The server (from the stabilization spine)

- **Removed tools (25 + `find_item_upgrades`):** listed in D-4. `find_item_upgrades` → `find_upgrades`.
  `save_tree` → `lua_save_build` (C-17). Old names return JSON-RPC unknown tool.
- **Env vars:**

  | Status | Variables |
  |---|---|
  | Removed | `POB_LUA_ENABLED`, `POE_TRADE_ENABLED`, `POB_LEGACY_GEM_TOOLS`, `POE_TRADE_USER_AGENT`, `POE_RATE_LIMIT_PER_SECOND`, `POE_CACHE_TTL` |
  | Added | `POE_TRADE_DISABLED`, `POESESSID` (`POE_SESSION_ID` stays as an alias), `POE_LEAGUE`, `POB_ALLOW_UNPINNED`, `POB_SKIP_ENGINE` (tests), `POB_API_TEST_ACTIONS` (tests), `POB_TEST_ENGINE` (set by Jest `globalSetup`) |
  | Changed meaning | `POB_TIMEOUT_MS` is now per request, measured from the write. `POB_FORK_PATH` still names the checkout's `src/`, but its default is now `vendor/`. The **default `POB_DIRECTORY`** changes from `~/Documents/Path of Building/Builds` (and a macOS variant) to `~/Documents/Path of Building (PoE2)/Builds` |
  | Kept (unchanged) | `POB_DIRECTORY`, `POB_CMD`, `POB_ARGS`, `POB_DEBUG`, `POE_NINJA_DISABLED`, `POE_TRADE_BASE`, `POE_TRADE_REALM`, `XDG_STATE_HOME` (standard) |

- **State:** moves to C-16 in WU-22. No data migration (none exists for this user).
- **Bridge protocol:** v1 → v2 lands in WU-06. Its Lua side, and the TS readiness change, are in one merge.
  The v1 TS request path tolerates v2 replies until WU-07 (it ignores ids and extra fields), and WU-07
  then requires `apiVersion` major `2`.
- **Cutover:** each unit is one merge on `stabilization/foundation`. Roll back by reverting that merge.
  The user's MCP registration (D-16) happens at WU-20.

### 13.2 The build-guide (from the build-guide spine)

- **Backward compatibility.** The 77 tools keep their contracts except C-43…C-49, each with its
  compatibility note in §5.2; D-360's `find_path_to_node` and `get_nearby_nodes` (§5.2, the user's HG-12
  decision; built, WU-66); and the v1.6 changes (§5.1 and §5.2): `find_upgrades`' dated header and prefixed
  stops (D-372), the minion-skill arguments, displays and numbers (D-376, D-377, D-379), the refusals, left-out
  lists and warnings for locked nodes and legacy content (D-383, D-384), emotion prices (D-387) and the ranker's
  minion field (D-388). Nothing is removed or renamed, and default outputs stay, so the golden values hold
  (AC-B5). **amended by D-439 (amendment 11, as built)**, an exception to "nothing is renamed": `get_node_power`'s reply renames `bestSwap.branch` to `branches[]` (amendment 11 §7, Appendix A); the existing node-power tests pass with `branches` (AC-P79). Env: `POB_GUIDES_DIRECTORY` is added (WU-37). Bridge: additive actions and params,
  plus one internal param rename (D-222). `mise run test` gains a separate NFR step (C-19).
- **Data.** No migration. New `data/` in the repo; the guides root is created by the first run; the state
  dir gains the write ledger at the first final compile (D-211). The run log moved from schema version 1
  to 2 (D-270) and to 3 with WU-58 (D-289); the checker reads a schema-2 log under its own log rules only,
  so WU-47's recorded runs, whose artifacts predate WU-62's formats, no longer pass (D-326 as D-341
  restates it). The plan's `schemaVersion` moved to 2 with WU-61 (D-320), and the record's `recordVersion`
  to 2 with WU-55 (D-321); version-1 plans stay accepted (D-332). Since WU-65 (D-351, D-358) the plan's
  `schemaVersion` is 3, with version 2 still accepted (AC-P60), and the record gains `measures` (D-353); since
  WU-78 (D-377) it is 4, versions 1 to 3 still accepted, each keeping its own shape. WU-65 recomputed the
  pipeline fixtures' `buildSha1` for the projection without the ledger, and D-386's projection refreshed the
  five fixture records whose hash matched their plan. Since WU-73 a schema-3 log needs `displays[]` (D-367), so
  run A's and the lean run's logs no longer pass `[display]`; the checker tests' helper rebuilds it. Since WU-81
  the checker requires the deck header, so `run-ok`, `run-ok-online` and `run-escalated` were migrated to the
  new deck and Pitch format; `run-ok-v2` is unchanged. `data/economy/prices.json` is new (D-373); prices in a
  plan stay as seen, so a re-price needs no migration. **Since amendment 8** (§13.3): plan schemas 5 to 10, older versions still accepted; the run log at schema 4 with skill revisions 11 to 14; the write ledger at version 2; `recordVersion` still 2.
- **Pipeline files.** `.claude/workflows/`, the workflow harness, `references/fallback.md` and the

## 14. Test and verification strategy

### 14.1 The server (from the stabilization spine)

**Test classes:**

| Class | Location | What it covers | Allowed fakes | Runs in |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Unit | `tests/unit/` (+ 4 legacy root suites) | pure TS: limiter, credentials, classification, parsers, currency, pin check, prefit, path canonicalization | fakes of our own collaborators, only as the AC names them; fake clock | `pnpm test` |
| In-process MCP | `tests/mcp/` | C-1 behaviour through `InMemoryTransport` + `Client` | real `Client`, router, handlers and session; HTTP only via `fixtureFetch`; a mock `spawn` only where the AC names it | `pnpm test` |
| Engine-required | `tests/integration/` | real luajit + `vendor/` pin + golden build: C-2, C-3, C-5, C-13 and every engine AC | real luajit and bridge; HTTP via `fixtureFetch` | `pnpm test`; **fails** without the engine unless `POB_SKIP_ENGINE=1` |
| Lua-direct | `tests/integration/` | the C-2 wire protocol, spoken by a minimal line client | real luajit | as engine-required |
| Stdio e2e | `tests/e2e/` | `build/index.js` spawned via `spawnServer`: stdin-EOF exit, stdout purity (NFR-8) | none (trade and ninja disabled by env) | `pnpm test` (after `pnpm build`, which `mise run test` does) |
| Static | `tests/static/` | I-5 cruft scan, env-var grep (D-14, D-25), README tool table (AC-G1), test-spawn env check | — | `pnpm test` |
| Script check | the verifier, recorded in the work-log | AC-F2, F6, F7, F8, F9, F17, AC-G4 | — | by hand, with exit codes |
| Human | WU-20, WU-21 | live trade, Windows PoB2 interplay, registration | — | the user |

**Fidelity rule.** A test that claims an AC must assert the *spec's* behaviour across the real seam its
class names, using only that class's allowed fakes. Every HTTP-sending AC asserts the requests that
`fixtureFetch.calls` recorded.

**Unit gate.** Before a unit is `done`, an independent verifier (not the doer) confirms:
1. `mise run test` exits 0 and `pnpm build` exits 0 (exit codes captured);
2. every AC in the unit's `acceptance` list maps to a named test (or a recorded script check) that
   passes and meets the fidelity rule for its class;
3. a code-diff review finds no correctness defects;
4. the hygiene allowlist and the cruft waivers changed only by removals of entries that now pass, plus
   additions this spec authorizes (D-45);
5. no file outside the unit's `touches` (plus implied scope, §10) changed, except the shared files under
   the serialized-wiring rule.

**Non-functional requirements** (each owned by an AC):

| NFR | Threshold | Condition | Owner AC |
|---|---|---|---|
| NFR-1 | Engine cold start to the first `ping` reply ≤ 5 s | this box, vendor pin | AC-E8 |
| NFR-2 | `checkPin()` ≤ 1 s | full vendor checkout | AC-F3 |
| NFR-3 | `find_upgrades` ≤ 15 s end to end | golden build, Body Armour, fixture fetch, no limiter waits | AC-T40 |
| NFR-4 | `find_upgrades` returns ≤ 90 s (+1 s) after the call arrives | any conditions, including lock waits and hung steps | AC-T34 |
| NFR-5 | `trade_generate_query` ≤ 8 s | any supported slot, golden build | AC-T22 |
| NFR-6 | Post-change snapshot ≤ 100 ms | golden build | AC-E22 |
| NFR-7 | `tools/list` answered ≤ 1 s after `initialize` | `fetch` and `spawn` mocked to fail | AC-F10 |
| NFR-8 | Every stdout line is a valid JSON-RPC 2.0 message | stdio e2e session, including an engine call | AC-F12 |
| NFR-9 | Zero trade requests sent that would exceed any window's `max − 1` | limiter simulation over fixture headers | AC-T5 |
| NFR-10 | `find_upgrades` output ≤ 30,000 characters | `limit: 20`, `include_non_upgrades: true`, fixtures | AC-T40 |

### 14.2 The build-guide (from the build-guide spine)

**Test classes.** The stab spine's classes (§14.1) apply unchanged (unit, in-process MCP, engine-required,
Lua-direct, stdio e2e, static, script check, human), with the same allowed fakes and the fidelity rule.
This epic adds:

| Class | Where | Covers | Runs in |
|---|---|---|---|
| Plan fixtures | `tests/fixtures/plans/` with engine-required tests | C-31…C-33, C-41, C-50: the two-stage plan (M-11), schema and per-stage errors, idempotence, the write ledger | `mise run test`; the fifteen-stage NFR test in its separate `--runInBand` step (C-19) |
| Static (pipeline files) | `tests/static/` over `.claude/skills/build-guide/` and `.claude/agents/build-guide/` | the skill names the four agent types and every step's type (D-267); allowlists name no trade or mutating tool outside the coordinator (D-178), and no poe.ninja tool beyond the coordinator's `get_currency_rates` (D-335) | `mise run test` |
| Pipeline run | WU-47, recorded in the work-log with the run-folder listing; WU-59, recorded the same way with an open brief (A-33; P1 and run A, D-361); WU-72, the lean run (D-361), recorded with telemetry (WU-64) | C-36 end to end, offline | the coordinator; an independent verifier checks the record |
| Human | WU-48 | the live run and play-test | the user |

**Unit gate.** The stab spine's five checks (§14.1), by an independent verifier, with §10.2's touches and wiring
list.

**Regression coverage for existing behaviour.** The existing suites stay and must pass unchanged except
for the count literals (§3.2.3): golden and hygiene tests, `engineKinds.test.ts`, `contract.test.ts`, the
README table test, the static scans, the ranker tests (AC-E39 order), and the fresh-clone check.

**Cross-cutting acceptance criteria** (owned here; slices cite them):
- **AC-B1 (tool surface).** After WU-41, `tools/list` returns 84 tools with every group enabled and 83
  with `POE_NINJA_DISABLED=true`; the set equals `tool-disposition.json`'s non-delete, non-pending names;
  the README table has 84 rows equal to `pnpm docs:tools` output. At each earlier tool-adding merge the
  same holds for that merge's count. The counts stay 84 and 83 through M-18: D-335 withdrew the poe.ninja
  tools (C-54, WU-52), and no later unit adds a tool, so no unit restates them. **amended by D-417 (amendment 9, as built)**: `get_node_power` (WU-92) raises the counts to 85 and 84 (amendment 9 §11); M-27's milestone gate counted 85 tools, 84 without poe.ninja (WL:6426–6441).
- **AC-B2 (hygiene).** At every unit gate, `tool-hygiene-allowlist.json` and `poe1-cruft-waivers.json`
  are `[]`. Every new tool has at least one `expect:"ok"` sample, a description of ≤ 2048 chars, and
  passes the output-text checks. The cruft, advice-threshold, `deletedPlumbing` and dead-module scans
  pass.
- **AC-B3 (engine kinds).** For every ok sample of a new or amended engine tool, a `read` tool leaves the
  canonical export identical and `revision` unchanged, and a `mutate` or `load` tool changes both.
  This includes `lua_get_stats` breaking down a non-main stat set, `lookup_game_data` (D-176), and both
  `guide_run` modes; its run-folder mode also never starts the engine (D-216).
- **AC-B4 (wire contract).** Every new Lua action has a C-5 schema, a typed bridge wrapper and a
  `contract.test.ts` case, added in the same merge.
- **AC-B5 (golden).** The 109 `PlayerStat` pairs, `golden-stat-fields.json`, CombinedDPS 104489.78 and
  the fresh-clone Life 2,977 are unchanged.
- **AC-B6 (offline harness).** Jest's `globalSetup` points `POB_GUIDES_DIRECTORY` at a fresh temp dir,
  `spawnServer` passes it explicitly, only `src/index.ts` reads it, and no test sends HTTP outside
  `fixtureFetch`.
- **AC-B7 (dependencies).** `package.json`'s dependency sections and `pnpm-lock.yaml` are byte-identical
  to the epic's base commit at every unit gate.
- **AC-B8 (write roots).** `guide_run` writes only the run folders it creates (D-207, D-216).
  `compile_plan` writes only files beside the plan inside the guides root, the target PoB file (through
  C-17; never in measure-only mode, D-208) and that file's write-ledger entry in the state dir (D-211).
  A `plan_path` that escapes the root (by `..`, an absolute path, or a symlink resolving outside), a
  `new_run` slug that isn't one safe path segment, or a guides root overlapping the builds folder
  (D-220) is `isError`, and nothing is written. **as built (D-479, amendment 14; `c5e3597`, WU-138)**: a final compile's writes also hold its copy beside the plan (`final-build.xml`).

**Non-functional requirements:**

| NFR | Threshold | Condition | Owner |
|---|---|---|---|
| NFR-20 | A 15-stage plan compiles in ≤ 100 s, one call (D-159, D-229) | this machine, vendor pin; the test runs alone, in `mise run test`'s `--runInBand` step (C-19) | WU-30 measures; an AC-P asserts |
| NFR-21 | Every per-stage engine request finishes in under 20 s, a third below the 30 s request timeout (D-47, D-156, D-229). Amended by amendment 13 (D-474, HG-27): `compile_stage` runs as a long action at 120 s, so its bound is 120 s (the Titan Rake run's two 30 s misses were HG-13's case); the 15-stage plan's 100 s total (AC-P37) stays | the same plan | AC-P |
| NFR-22 | Zero 429 responses and zero `locked_out` refusals during pricing (D-160) | the M-13 live run, from its run log: no pricing step's `priceOutcomes` counts a `rate_limited` or `locked_out` call (D-218, D-270) | AC-W, WU-48 |
| NFR-23 | ≤ 3 concepts per research round, 3 V4 lenses per pass, ≤ 2 counted retries per gate, fan-out ≤ 3 (D-127, D-161, D-235, D-267, D-272). Since WU-57 and WU-58 (D-287): per brainstorm round ≤ 3 R1a researchers and one R1a-assemble, 6–9 cards dealt (or a short round with its reason, D-339); per verification round ≤ 3 cards compiled. The probe budget, up to 4 one-stage variants per card, is D-292's separate rule, not an NFR-23 cap. **Amended by D-352 (v1.5; built, WU-69):** one V4 pass per cycle, with no lenses, in place of three lenses per pass. **Amended by D-390 (v1.6; built, WU-82):** a gate's question steps (stage `Q`) run up to 3 research steps in parallel, and a coordinator step alone, so fan-out stays ≤ 3. **amended by D-429 (amendment 10, as built, M-23)**: a brainstorm round's coordinator step, `R1a-synthesize`, composes 1–4 composites, in place of 6–9 cards dealt (amendment 10 §4) | any run, from its `stages[]` and the session transcript (D-270) | AC-W |
| NFR-24 | *Withdrawn → D-316:* the numeric cap on new tools is lifted | — | — |

## 15. Constraints and non-goals

### 15.1 The server (from the stabilization spine)

- **Runtimes:**
  - Node 24 (no move to ≥ 25 while relying on corepack);
  - pnpm 11.6.0;
  - LuaJIT ref `2460b3ff` + luautf8 0.1.6-1, via mise.
- **Pinned dependencies:**
  - PoB2 `v0.23.1` (`7d6f530`), **read-only**: no PoB files are modified, and `pob-api/` is our only Lua;
  - `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` 1.20.2, no upgrade in this epic;
  - zod 3.
- **Platform and layout:**
  - code lives under `~/projects/pob2-mcp`;
  - the user's builds are on case-insensitive drvfs `/mnt/c`;
  - WSL2.
- **Outbound hosts:**
  - pathofexile.com (trade2) and poe.ninja, at runtime only, when the user calls a tool;
  - pobb.in and pastebin (`import_build`);
  - github.com (pin fetch).
- **Secrets:** the user's `.env` holds `POESESSID` in plaintext. This is a HUMAN exception for this
  project only. Nothing else is persisted.

### 15.2 The build-guide (from the build-guide spine)

**Constraints:**
- **The stab spine's constraints (§15.1) hold:** Node 24, pnpm 11.6.0, LuaJIT via mise; SDK 1.20.2; zod 3; PoB2
  v0.23.1 read-only (game 0.5.4 data); text-only tool output (D-36); code under `~/projects/pob2-mcp`.
- **No new dependencies** (HG-10): no YAML parser, no runtime JSON-Schema validator, no expression
  library.
- **PoE2 only**, bound to the pin.
- **The ten constraints D-105…D-114**, and the standing rules: no secrets in files, logs or argv; trade
  and poe.ninja only through the server; builds-folder writes only through `buildFileWriter`; no network
  in tests; no agent reads `.env`.
- **Cost is a stated concern:** fan-out stays within D-127 and D-161. Since WU-57 (D-287), a card the user
  doesn't want stops at a gate before finalization. Since M-15 (D-349, D-357): a run like run A without
  faults uses at most 1M output tokens by telemetry (WU-64), a final-gate revision reported beside it; each
  agent's peak context is reported against a design target of 150K, and the cache read per step is
  reported. The lean run used about 1.20M before its revision; the user waived the miss and holds the lever
  (2026-10-14). Amendment 7's gate question steps add agents to a run, a cost the user accepted for the
  interviews (R-46). **amended by D-416 (amendment 9, as built)**: targets by phase per build replace the one 1M cap (amendment 9 §10); amendment 10 §11 (the user's choice 7) raises the shared phases' target from 1.2M to 1.3M output tokens for composites. Later cost estimates are in §15.3.

**Non-goals** (design): dedicated boss-killer builds; an encounter-specific target input; live tracking
of the player's character; an in-game overlay or automation; hosting or publishing guides; re-checking
guides after game patches; PoE1; perfect simulation (hand calculations are accepted and labelled). From
amendment 3 (D-302): an interaction database or graph; hunting for undocumented interactions; modelling
mechanics in Lua beyond Count and custom modifiers, or patching `vendor/` (**amended by D-492**, amendment 14, marked by D-496: the bridge corrects PoB's parse of the lines `pob-api/API/ParseFixes.lua` lists; `vendor/` stays unmodified); scoring candidates by formula;
required gates from the verification checklist (**amended by D-436**, amendment 11, marked by D-496: a `damage-missing` card fails its attempt and retries). From the user's decisions after run A: crafting guidance
(D-348; **amended by D-471 (amendment 13, as built)**: one exception, corruption on cheap uniques and +1-level skill gems on `b` and `n`, which D-484 (amendment 14) widens to an extra socket on a unique that isn't cheap), boss kill-time modelling (D-347), and campaign resistance or defence calculations (D-346). From
amendment 5: pruning a build for its price (D-371). Also out
of this epic: plugin packaging, per-agent
engines, unique-jewel trade rows (HG-15), a pin bump (HG-11), the pipeline's own price cache (D-169), and
the deferred items: the vendor search regex (HG-19), rolls above the data range (HG-20), finalizing
non-skill damage sources (HG-21) and a trade-link field (HG-22; **no longer deferred**: D-488 (amendment 14, as built) settles HG-22: every rare slot of `b` and `n` gets a weighted trade link).

### 15.3 Since amendment 7 (amendments 8–16)

Constraints and non-goals from amendments 8–16 (16's M-29 built, its M-30 approved, not built), by amendment, each with its source; a change that amends a rule above sits beside that rule as a note instead. "Amendment N §n" is a section of that amendment's file (`docs/build-guide/spec/amendment-N-*.md`); worklog:n and WL:n are lines of `docs/build-guide/worklog.md`; "handoff M-nn" is that milestone's section of `docs/build-guide/build-handoff-amendments.md`; a hash is a signed commit on `main`.

- **Amendment 8, finding the build's engine (M-20).**
  - Constraints: poe2wiki stays out (it refuses agents behind a bot shield); video transcripts set aside; nothing runs the digest on a schedule; no price in the index (a unique's price is popularity plus rarity) (amendment 8 §1, §2, §3); facts live in `data/` (`data/league/`, `data/rules/`, `data/monsters/`) and don't move if the skill splits, how to reason stays in the skill, `pob-gaps.md` stays where it is, and amendment 8's files are written so the split's move needs only path changes (amendment 8 §12).
- **Amendment 9, endgame builds first, and trees that minimize travel (M-21, M-22).**
  - Constraints: no scratch builds for researchers (FR-W3); researchers' data questions go through the tools, Grep in PoB's `Modules/` only for how the engine computes (D-425); no respec list from the user's own PoB file for now (the user's ruling, amendment 9 §1).
- **Amendment 10, one build from the whole brainstorm (M-23).**
  - Constraints: cost, the shared phases' target is 1.3M output tokens (amendment 10 §11, choice 7, amending D-416's 1.2M); the estimate was about +163K, about 1.26M (amendment 10 §11)
  - Constraints: the pattern library's 13,000-word cap (amendment 9 §24.1) holds questions 16–19 (amendment 10 §6); 12,646 words at WU-107's merge (worklog:5531)
- **Amendment 11, builds a player would run (M-24).**
  - Constraints: cost, about −25K to +65K output tokens on a two-build run, the rival's removal saving about 85K; no target change (amendment 11 §15)
  - Constraints: caps held at the merges: `SKILL.md` 325 of 325 lines, `patterns.md` 12,644 of 13,000 words (worklog:5824), `get_node_power`'s description 2,023 of 2,048 characters (worklog:5796)
  - D-436's exception to D-302's "required gates from the verification checklist" is marked as an amendment in §15.2's non-goals (D-496).
  - Neither the toughness band nor the boss floor fails a gate; both show trades for the user to decide (amendment 11 §9, §9b)
- **Amendment 12, defence that holds, one tree per loadout, and the design's questions (M-25).**
  - Constraints and non-goals: G2 (a max-hit ranking stat for node power) deferred, to revisit if a live run meets a one-shot that node power's EHP ranking and pool lines can't fix (amendment 12 §1, §3); PG-85 (block against spells) dropped, trusting PoB, and block is out of the hits-survived metric (the user's metric is evade or deflect) (amendment 12 §1, Names); D9 already done (amendment 12 §3); no respec gold figure (A-32); the deflected-ratio pass changes `data.misc` at run time only and restores it, `vendor/` unmodified (amendment 12 §4); no allowlist entry for B5 (amendment 12 §15); `SKILL.md` within 325 lines, routes paid for by trims (amendment 12 §19, §25). Cost (amendment 12 §18): about +170K to +270K output tokens on a two-build run with no failing rung, more with one-shot and failed rungs; the pace floor costs no tokens but at least 15 minutes of wall time (R-53).
- **Amendment 13, a tree search judged on targets, the finishing touches, and the run's rough edges (M-26).**
  - Constraints: D-348's one exception (D-471). Non-goals added: timeless jewels (PG-102), budget tiers, Headhunter and Mageblood, a second anoint (the Raven-Touched Shard, 196 divines), filled charm slots (D-473, D-472, amendment 13 §1). `SKILL.md`'s line cap: 325, as built 331, the 6 lines named in the work-log (worklog:6136–6139). Cost (amendment 13 §18): about +200K to +400K output a two-build run, more when the swap loop runs to its cap; D-349's 1M (targets by phase since D-416) is already exceeded by today's runs, the added cost the user's to weigh, D-357's knobs theirs
- **Amendment 14, a PoB file at every gate, items as specs, and the tree tools' repairs (M-27).**
  - Constraints: the deferred list's "a trade-link field (HG-22)" is no longer deferred (D-488). Amendment 14 §22's cost: about +150K to +300K output tokens on a two-build run, less a swap's saving; D-349's 1M cap (targets by phase since D-416) was already exceeded by today's runs and the user weighed the cost at approval. `SKILL.md` stands at 349 lines (the user set 325 at amendment 11; told to the user, WL:6413). The bridge's first correction of PoB's parse (D-492) sits beside D-302's non-goal (cross-referenced beside the non-goal in §15.2, not amended).
- **Amendment 15, the plugin layout, the shared rules and game knowledge (M-28; built 2026-11-06).**
  - Constraints (a15 §1, §10, §11, §12): this environment only (WSL2, Ubuntu 24.04); native Windows and macOS are designed for and on the roadmap, not built, so the amendments add no POSIX-only dependency beyond `bin/` and the reminder, and script code builds paths with Node's `path` (D-506). `/pob2:build-guide`'s `SKILL.md` at most 349 lines at M-28 and under 325 by the split's end; each piece's under 150; each stage's words loaded at most 5% over its baseline (D-505). Cost: 2M to 4M subagent tokens for the build (a15 §12). Non-goals of this amendment: review (amendment 17), the server's packaging (amendment 18), and amendment 14's deferrals (charge generation, charms, timeless jewels, the trade pacing's tuning).
  - As built: `/pob2:build-guide`'s `SKILL.md` 317 lines, game knowledge's 121 (WL:6661); every stage's words loaded within its bound, `max +0.2%` on the merged tree (WL:6731–6751); the build's token cost isn't in the work-log at this fold.
- **Amendment 16, the pieces that start from a PoB file, and launch day (M-29, M-30; approved 2026-11-08; M-29 built 2026-11-09, M-30 not built).**
  - Constraints (a16 §1, §21, §23, §28): M-29 must merge well before 1.0 starts, 2026-12-11 20:00 UTC, and before M-30 starts. Each piece's `SKILL.md` fewer than 150 lines, its description at most 1,536 characters; `/pob2:build-guide`'s under 325 at both milestones' gates; each stage's words loaded within 5% of its baseline (D-513). This environment only, as amendment 15's (D-506). Cost (a16 §23, subagent tokens): M-29 about 3M to 5M; M-30 about 7M to 11M, the largest milestone yet (R-126); WU-168's agent runs about 1.5M to 2.5M output, 200M to 350M cache read and 5M to 8M cache write. Non-goals of this amendment (a16 §29, and amendment 14's header): PG-115's patch or upstream issue, PG-112, PG-65's fix and the pipeline's side of PG-111; review, discovery and research (amendment 17); the server in the plugin, its setup and the live test (amendment 18); charge generation, charms, timeless jewels and the trade pacing's tuning, still deferred.
  - As built at M-29 (WL:7148, :7052–7053, :7082, :7093, :7186, :7197, :7305): `/pob2:build-guide`'s `SKILL.md` 317 lines, game knowledge's 125; every stage's words loaded within 5% of its baseline at each merge; the doers' subagent tokens 0.29M (WU-156), 0.49M (WU-151), 0.29M (WU-152), 0.29M (WU-153), 0.40M (WU-154) and 0.45M (WU-155), about 2.2M before the verifiers, reviewers and fix rounds, which the work-log doesn't total; PG-115 checked and not recorded, so its patch or issue stays a non-goal with nothing to carry.

status line records its approval; the files stay as the rationale record, and this spine (with the slices, as far as §4
says) is authoritative. Amendments 1 to 7 were folded into the build-guide spine (v1.3 to v1.6); amendments 8 to 14 are
folded here, on 2026-11-04; amendment 15's rows entered as approved on 2026-11-06 and WU-149 marked them built the same
day, but D-506, which stays approved as the roadmap; amendment 16's rows entered as approved on 2026-11-08, and WU-158
marked M-29's built on 2026-11-09. The milestones' records are in §9, the live tests' state in §1.2.
